Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the functional_analysis object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 805. The moduli space has dimension at most 363 with 5 connected components.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 805 + O(1/n^363). Summing over all modes and applying the functional_analysis convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.
